<meta name="csrf-token" content="OHEH9hXiLqSKoMKMuX2mZlAz0IkE5CfDUN880Xp5ox-qFIJ1QKniJZGtQfcm9JkhMohCY6qR6Hqw20qMqIi4Pg" />
<script>
(function() {
  // Only run on product detail pages — check for the branch.io meta tag
  var meta = document.querySelector('meta[name="branch:deeplink:$deeplink_path"]');
  if (!meta || !/^product\/details\/\d+/.test(meta.getAttribute('content') || '')) {
    return;
  }

  // Custom copy shown when the login modal appears via the add-to-cart flow.
  // Restored to the default copy if the user closes and reopens the modal from elsewhere.
  var CUSTOM_HEADLINE = 'ใส่ตะกร้าไว้จ่ายทีหลังได้';
  var CUSTOM_SUBHEAD = 'กรอกเบอร์มือถือ เพื่อกลับมาซื้อหรือใช้โค้ดลดได้ทุกเมื่อ';
  var DEFAULT_HEADLINE = 'ให้เรื่องสุขภาพเป็นเรื่องจิ๊บๆ';

  var clickEl = function(el) {
    el.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent('click', {
      bubbles: true, cancelable: true, view: window
    }));
  };

  var isLoggedOut = function() {
    var loginBtn = document.querySelector('[data-target="#login_modal"]');
    return loginBtn && loginBtn.offsetParent !== null;
  };

  var customizeLoginModal = function() {
    var titleEl = document.querySelector('.auth__title');
    if (!titleEl) return false;
    if (titleEl.dataset.customized === '1') return true;

    titleEl.textContent = CUSTOM_HEADLINE;

    if (!titleEl.parentElement.querySelector('.auth__subtitle--custom')) {
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'auth__subtitle--custom';
      sub.textContent = CUSTOM_SUBHEAD;
      sub.style.cssText = 'font-size:15px;color:#6b7280;margin-top:12px;margin-bottom:24px;font-weight:400;line-height:1.5;';
      titleEl.insertAdjacentElement('afterend', sub);
    }

    titleEl.dataset.customized = '1';
    return true;
  };

  var restoreLoginModal = function() {
    var titleEl = document.querySelector('.auth__title');
    if (titleEl) {
      titleEl.textContent = DEFAULT_HEADLINE;
      delete titleEl.dataset.customized;
    }
    var sub = document.querySelector('.auth__subtitle--custom');
    if (sub) sub.remove();
  };

  var handler = function(e) {
    var addBtn = e.target.closest && e.target.closest('.js-add-to-cart-btn');
    if (!addBtn) return;
    if (!isLoggedOut()) return;

    var stage = 'waiting-for-view-cart';
    var done = false;
    var modifiedElements = [];

    // Dim overlay: sits between cart modal (z-index 1040) and login modal (z-index 1050)
    var overlay = document.createElement('div');
    overlay.id = '__auto-checkout-dim';
    overlay.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(0,0,0,0.5);z-index:1045;pointer-events:none;opacity:0;transition:opacity 200ms ease;';
    document.body.appendChild(overlay);
    requestAnimationFrame(function() { overlay.style.opacity = '1'; });

    var removeOverlay = function() {
      if (overlay.parentNode) {
        overlay.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(function() { overlay.remove(); }, 200);
      }
    };

    // Demote cart + package-selection modals so the dim overlay covers them
    var demoteObserver = new MutationObserver(function() {
      document.querySelectorAll('.modal.shopping-cart, .shopping-cart.modal, [class*="package-selection"].modal, .modal[class*="package-selection"]').forEach(function(el) {
        if (el.style.zIndex !== '1040') {
          modifiedElements.push({ el: el, original: el.style.zIndex });
          el.style.zIndex = '1040';
        }
      });
      // Try customizing the login modal as soon as it appears in the DOM
      customizeLoginModal();
    });
    demoteObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true });

    var restore = function() {
      modifiedElements.forEach(function(item) { item.el.style.zIndex = item.original; });
      modifiedElements.length = 0;
      removeOverlay();
      restoreLoginModal();
    };

    // When the login modal is dismissed, clean up everything
    var cleanupObserver = new MutationObserver(function() {
      var telInput = document.querySelector('input[type="tel"]');
      var loginVisible = telInput && telInput.offsetParent !== null;
      if (!loginVisible && stage === 'done') {
        restore();
        demoteObserver.disconnect();
        cleanupObserver.disconnect();
      }
    });

    // Stage machine: wait for view-cart button, click it, wait for checkout button, click it
    var observer, pollInterval;
    var tick = function() {
      if (stage === 'waiting-for-view-cart') {
        var viewCartBtn = document.querySelector('.js-open-cart-btn');
        if (viewCartBtn && !viewCartBtn.disabled) {
          stage = 'waiting-for-checkout';
          setTimeout(function() { clickEl(viewCartBtn); }, 100);
        }
      } else if (stage === 'waiting-for-checkout') {
        var checkoutBtn = document.querySelector('.shopping-cart__footer__cta__buttons--default');
        if (checkoutBtn && !checkoutBtn.disabled) {
          stage = 'done';
          done = true;
          observer.disconnect();
          clearInterval(pollInterval);
          setTimeout(function() {
            clickEl(checkoutBtn);
            // Try customizing repeatedly in case modal renders after click
            var customizeInterval = setInterval(function() {
              if (customizeLoginModal()) clearInterval(customizeInterval);
            }, 50);
            setTimeout(function() { clearInterval(customizeInterval); }, 3000);
            setTimeout(function() {
              cleanupObserver.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true });
            }, 1000);
          }, 200);
        }
      }
    };

    observer = new MutationObserver(tick);
    observer.observe(document.body, { childList: true, subtree: true, attributes: true });
    pollInterval = setInterval(tick, 200);

    // Safety: give up after 8 seconds if the flow stalls
    setTimeout(function() {
      if (!done) {
        observer.disconnect();
        clearInterval(pollInterval);
        demoteObserver.disconnect();
        restore();
      }
    }, 8000);
  };

  // Attach click listener in capture phase, after DOM is ready
  var attach = function() {
    document.addEventListener('click', handler, true);
  };
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', attach);
  } else {
    attach();
  }
})();
</script></head>
